2026-11-02T06:16:05.1291724Z   UV_CACHE_DIR: /home/runner/work/_temp/setup-uv-cache
2026-11-02T06:16:05.1296076Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [require-build] [cluster:firmus] [nodes:2] [prefill-pp4] Serve PP4 round 23 with the V tile writer gate enabled on top of P and the Decode greedy fusion

This commit turns on prefill.fa3_v_writer_gate, so the job builds a new image
that contains patches/sglang/prefill-v-writer.patch and then serves through
the usual path.

W keeps the old FP32 writer's Q and K CTAs and adds V-only CTAs in the same
launch. Each V CTA covers 96 tokens of one head of one request. It writes
the paged V and the native V image directly with contiguous 16-byte stores,
so the contiguous V intermediate and the separate pack launch leave the
path. That saves 8,179,712 bytes of V write and read per layer at
3994+3994. The CPU admission is recorded in
research/runtime-performance/2026-11-02-prefill-v-writer-r23.md: byte-equal
layout and FP8 conversion, 48 registers, no spill, occupancy unchanged at 5
CTAs per SM, and unchanged SASS for every path that does not use W.

The W gate runs after the P and P3 gate and after the Decode greedy gate,
when at least 760 seconds remain. It compares the writer to FA3 to O
composite against P on 12 Prefill GPUs and 7 pair shapes. It requires
byte-equal Q, K, paged K and V, native V image, attention, scale and O, plus
1200 endurance calls. It adopts W only when 3994+3994 is at least 2 us
faster on every GPU and no shape regresses by more than 1 us. Otherwise the
P or P3 selection stays. W plus P3 is not measured, so it is never served.

Expected gain is 2 to 5 us per layer, about 4 to 11 tok/s, as a
conditional estimate. The serving test now expects the gate on.

This commit turns on prefill.fa3_v_writer_gate, so the job builds a new image
that contains patches/sglang/prefill-v-writer.patch and then serves through
the usual path.

W keeps the old FP32 writer''s Q and K CTAs and adds V-only CTAs in the same
launch. Each V CTA covers 96 tokens of one head of one request. It writes
the paged V and the native V image directly with contiguous 16-byte stores,

path. That saves 8,179,712 bytes of V write and read per layer at
3994+3994. The CPU admission is recorded in
research/runtime-performance/2026-11-02-prefill-v-writer-r23.md: byte-equal
layout and FP8 conversion, 48 registers, no spill, occupancy unchanged at 5
CTAs per SM, and unchanged SASS for every path that does not use W.

This commit turns on prefill.fa3_v_writer_gate, so the job builds a new image
that contains patches/sglang/prefill-v-writer.patch and then serves through
the usual path.

W keeps the old FP32 writer''s Q and K CTAs and adds V-only CTAs in the same
launch. Each V CTA covers 96 tokens of one head of one request. It writes
the paged V and the native V image directly with contiguous 16-byte stores,
so the contiguous V intermediate and the separate pack launch leave the
path. That saves 8,179,712 bytes of V write and read per layer at
3994+3994. The CPU admission is recorded in
research/runtime-performance/2026-11-02-prefill-v-writer-r23.md: byte-equal
layout and FP8 conversion, 48 registers, no spill, occupancy unchanged at 5
CTAs per SM, and unchanged SASS for every path that does not use W.
